Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on bayesian_statistics structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 392. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 432 with relations in degree 5.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 392 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the bayesian_statistics structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 432. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
